enabled still emits the initial snapshot and then stays silent.

```ts
type BotEventFilter = Message<"soulfire.v1.BotEventFilter"> & {
  /**
   * Deliver position / rotation / health / food / xp / dimension / game mode
   * changes as BotStateDelta.
   *
   * @generated from field: bool include_state_deltas = 1;
   */
  includeStateDeltas: boolean;

   * Deliver incoming chat and system messages as BotChatEvent.
   *
   * @generated from field: bool include_chat = 2;
   */
  includeChat: boolean;

  /**
   * Deliver connect / disconnect / spawn / death / respawn events as
   * BotLifecycleEvent.
   *
   * @generated from field: bool include_lifecycle = 3;
   */
  includeLifecycle: boolean;

  /**
   * Deliver entity spawn / update / despawn within entity_radius as
   * BotEntityEvent. Setting this without a positive radius defaults to 32
   * blocks to keep the stream manageable.
   *
   * @generated from field: bool include_entity_events = 4;
   */
  includeEntityEvents: boolean;

  /**
   * Maximum radius (blocks) for entity events. Ignored if
   * include_entity_events is false. Server clamps to a sane upper bound.
   *
   * @generated from field: float entity_radius = 5;
   */

  /**
   * Deliver block change events within block_radius around the bot as
   * BotBlockUpdateEvent. Setting this without a positive radius defaults to
   * 16 blocks.
   *
   * @generated from field: bool include_block_updates = 6;
   */
  includeBlockUpdates: boolean;

  /**
   * Maximum radius (blocks) for block updates. Ignored if
   * include_block_updates is false. Server clamps to a sane upper bound.
   *
   * @generated from field: float block_radius = 7;
   */
  blockRadius: number;

  /**
   * Deliver inventory and open-container snapshots when their state changes.
   *
   * @generated from field: bool include_inventory = 8;
   */
  includeInventory: boolean;

  /**
   * Deliver explicit damage events.
   *
   * @generated from field: bool include_damage = 9;
   */
  includeDamage: boolean;

  /**
   * Deliver world clock, weather, and other server game-state changes.
   *
   * @generated from field: bool include_environment = 10;
   */
  includeEnvironment: boolean;

  /**
   * Deliver tab-list additions, removals, and metadata updates.
   *
   * @generated from field: bool include_player_list = 11;
   */
  includePlayerList: boolean;

  /**
   * Deliver boss-bar lifecycle and property updates.
   *
   * @generated from field: bool include_boss_bars = 12;
   */
  includeBossBars: boolean;

  /**
   * Deliver sound playback and stop events.
   *
   * @generated from field: bool include_sounds = 13;
   */
  includeSounds: boolean;

  /**
   * Deliver particle spawn events.
   *
   * @generated from field: bool include_particles = 14;
   */
  includeParticles: boolean;

  /**
   * Deliver objectives, scores, display slots, and team updates.
   *
   * @generated from field: bool include_scoreboard = 15;
   */
  includeScoreboard: boolean;

  /**
   * Deliver server resource-pack offers and removals.
   *
   * @generated from field: bool include_resource_packs = 16;
   */

  /**
   * Deliver title, subtitle, timing, clear, and reset events.
   *
   * @generated from field: bool include_titles = 17;
   */

   */
```

### `includeEntityEvents`

Deliver entity spawn / update / despawn within entity_radius as
BotEntityEvent. Setting this without a positive radius defaults to 32
blocks to keep the stream manageable.

**Type:** `boolean`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_live_pb.ts#L167)

Maximum radius (blocks) for entity events. Ignored if
include_entity_events is false. Server clamps to a sane upper bound.

**Type:** `number`.

### `includeBlockUpdates`

Deliver block change events within block_radius around the bot as
BotBlockUpdateEvent. Setting this without a positive radius defaults to
16 blocks.

**Type:** `boolean`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_live_pb.ts#L184)

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_live_pb.ts#L192)

## BotLiveService

**Object** in `generated/soulfire/bot_live_pb`. [Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_live_pb.ts#L4360).

Live bot remote-control service. See file header for scope and design notes.

Permissions:
- READ_BOT_INFO: required for WatchBotEvents, GetBlock, FindBlocks,
                 ListNearbyEntities.
- CONTROL_BOT_ACTIONS: required for chat, interactions, pathfinding, and
                       exclusive control leases.

Error handling mirrors BotService:
- NOT_FOUND: instance or bot does not exist.
- FAILED_PRECONDITION: bot is not online, target not loaded, or the
                       requested action is not currently valid.
- OUT_OF_RANGE: target is outside the bot's reach / observation radius.
- INTERNAL: unexpected server errors.

**Import from:** `@soulfiremc/sdk/generated/soulfire/bot_live_pb`.

```ts
   *

   */
```
```ts
  entityId: number;
```

```ts
   */
  distance: number;

   */
  displayName?: string | undefined;

  /**
   * True if this entity is a player.

   */

};
```

Search quality and latency policy. Unset uses NORMAL.

**Type:** [`PathfindSearchMode`](/docs/sdk/api/ts/pathfind-search-mode).

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_live_pb.ts#L3296)

### `maximumQualityBound`

Largest accepted certified multiplicative quality bound. Values must be
from 1.0 through the selected mode's initial epsilon. Unset uses the mode
default. ARA* does not execute an incumbent that misses this bound.

**Type:** `number | undefined` (optional).

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_live_pb.ts#L3305)

### `smoothCamera`

Smooth camera movement only inside movement-specific safe tolerances.
Block interactions and precision movement remain deterministic.

**Type:** `boolean | undefined` (optional).

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_live_pb.ts#L3313)

### `maximumExpandedStates`

Hard state-expansion budget for the complete ARA* repair session. 0 uses
the server default. Reaching this limit is reported separately from an
unreachable goal.

**Type:** `number`.

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_live_pb.ts#L3322)

### `maximumFallDistance`

Largest ordinary fall onto solid ground. Values above 3 are rejected
because longer falls require a dedicated safe-landing primitive.

**Type:** `number | undefined` (optional).

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_live_pb.ts#L3330)

### `maximumParkourGap`

Largest unsupported horizontal gap that parkour may cross. Set to 0 to
disable parkour. Values above 3 are rejected by the current controller.

**Type:** `number | undefined` (optional).

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_live_pb.ts#L3338)

### `protectedBlocks`

Blocks the route may move around but must never break. Positions in a
different dimension are rejected instead of being silently reinterpreted
in the bot's current dimension.

**Type:** `BlockPosition[]` ([`BlockPosition`](/docs/sdk/api/ts/block-position)).

[Source](https://github.com/soulfiremc-com/SoulFire/blob/878ba24b85ee4a1ec170ed738b7664b61ace1a2d/sdk/typescript/src/generated/soulfire/bot_live_pb.ts#L3347)
